<details>
<summary>🧠 思考过程 (Thinking Process - 点击展开)</summary>

> **[分析语法错误]**
> 我需要仔细考虑。用户提到代码运行时抛出了错误，提示在某个位置遇到SyntaxError，期望Unicode转义序列\uXXXX。提供的代码行是：`if (hash.startsWith("#/claims/new")) return { name: "new" };` 用户怀疑存在未转义的字符或隐藏的Unicode字符，可能混用了不同的引号或中间有特殊字符转义错误。
>
> **[检查代码中的格式问题]**
> 我需要确认是否有非标准字符或隐藏的格式标记，可能是用了弯引号或不间断空格。代码中有"⚙️"表情符号，在注释中，不应该影响解析。错误出现在276行，列28，可能是因为"\n"的错误转义，实际是'/n'，而不是转义字符'\n'，因此问题不在这里。
>
> **[检查TSX标记中的转义字符]**
> 错误可能是由于字符串或模板字面量中的"\c"触发的。文档中有行：`<div className="mt-1 text-sm text-stone-600">`，出现了反斜杠转义双引号`\"`。但在TSX中，属性值中的引号只需使用普通的ASCII引号，不需要反斜杠转义。
>
> **[调整TSX中的转义字符]**
> 在TSX文件中，代码并不是字符串，因此添加反斜杠转义会导致无效的`\"`标记，可能被解析为反斜杠后跟引号。这会引起"期待Unicode转义序列"错误。解决方法是移除所有JSX属性值中的多余反斜杠，只保留正常的引号。
>
> **[处理错误和添加测试]**
> 为了修复错误，我们需要确保所有字符串格式正确，并移除非标准字符。JSX中必须使用ASCII引用符。在开发过程中，我们可以通过添加“自检”功能来进行简单测试，但不会影响UI显示。对于函数`parseRouteHash`，我们会引入纯函数，确保处理潜在无效字符，并根据需求进行排序测试。
>
</details>

## 22:17 外部文档站方案

来源：68ff7ee8-bfb4-8321-8185-68ac9c2319dd
时间：2025-10-27 22:17

### 我
<!-- mid: 15f30949-41ca-4486-805b-5b967449402c -->
结论：用外部文档站更省事。你可以采用“独立仓库 + 开源文档框架 + 静态托管”的方案；成本接近零。

# 可选方案与费用

* GitHub Pages（+Cloudflare Pages/Vercel/CDN）：免费。
* GitBook：有免费层，团队/自定义权限要付费。
* Read the Docs：开源免费，私有/高级功能付费。
* 自建（Nginx + 静态）：仅你的服务器成本。

# 优缺点

* 外部托管优点：省运维、CDN、版本管理、PR 审核、搜索友好、多人协作。
* 外部托管缺点：域名分离、埋点分散、样式需对齐品牌。
* 自建优点：完全控制、同域。
* 自建缺点：你来运维与扩容。

# 我给你的推荐（V1）

* 框架：**Docusaurus**（自带 i18n，适合中英双语）。
* 托管：**GitHub Pages** 或 **Cloudflare Pages**（免费）。
* 仓库：`leverageguard-docs`（独立于主仓库）。
* 域名：`help.yourdomain.com`（CNAME 到 Pages），站内 `/help` 301 跳转。
* 权限：PR 流改文档；主站仅放“帮助”链接。

# 最小落地清单

1. 建文档仓库，启用 Docusaurus 模板与 i18n（zh-CN/en）。
2. 写三块内容：快速开始、常见问题、理赔/证据说明。
3. 配 Pages（GitHub 或 Cloudflare），绑定子域。
4. 主站导航指向 `help.*`，保留一个离线兜底页（简单 FAQ）以防外部站故障。
5. 加搜索（可先用浏览器搜索；后续接 Algolia DocSearch，免费层）。

# 何时考虑 GitBook

* 需要非工程同学频繁编辑、评论、草稿流程；接受托管风格与可能的付费。

# 结论再说一次

* 第一版：外部静态文档站最优。几乎零费用，维护低。你可以立即采用。 ，做这个，

> For GitHub Pages:
> 1. Create `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` to build and deploy using GitHub Actions `actions/deploy-pages@v4`.
> 2. Set repo settings for Pages and custom domain if using CNAME.
> 
> For Cloudflare Pages, setup `wrangler.toml` and caching/security headers with `static/_headers`. Optionally, you can set up redirection to the help page.

> **[Configuring Redirection and Workflow]**
> To set up redirection, use `res.redirect(301, 'https://help.example.com' + req.path);`.
> 
> For the PR workflow, add a `CODEOWNERS` file for required reviews, set branch protections, and document commit guidelines in `docs/CONTRIBUTING.md`.
>

很好。按你描述，V1 已就绪。给你一份发布前核对清单和立即可做的三步升级。

# 发布前核对清单（必做）
- Pages 目标：仓库 Settings → Pages = GitHub Actions。
- Node 版本：Actions 固定 Node 20（你已配，确认无 warn）。
- 域名与路径：
  - 临时：`url=https://<user>.github.io`，`baseUrl=/leverageguard-docs/`
  - 绑定自域后：`url=https://help.<域名>`，`baseUrl=/`，并在 `static/CNAME` 写入域名。
- i18n：中文为 default，`i18n/en/...` 与中文一一对应。
- 301 跳转：主站 `/help` 永久重定向到 `https://help.<域名>`。
- 构建产物：`npm run build` 后本地 `npm run serve` 自检 200 与样式。
- 链接检查：跑一次 `npx linkinator build --recurse` 保证无 404。
- SEO：预发环境加 `static/robots.txt` → `Disallow: /`；生产放开。
- 权限：开启分支保护，要求 1 人 code review。
- 备份兜底：主站保留简版 FAQ 页面，外站挂了也能看。

# 两处常见踩坑
- CNAME 未生效或缓存未刷：等 5–30 分钟再测 HTTPS，必要时清浏览器缓存。
- `baseUrl` 忘改：改域名后仍用仓库路径会导致资源 404。生产一定用 `/`。

# 立刻可做的三步升级（推荐）
1) **搜索**：申请 Algolia DocSearch（免费层）。获批后在 `themeConfig.algolia` 填 `appId/apiKey/indexName`，触发重建即可生效。  
2) **监测与分析**：接入 Plausible 或 Cloudflare Web Analytics，无 Cookie，直接在 `<head>` 加脚本即可。  
3) **团队协作**：新增 `.github/CODEOWNERS`、`PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md`，规范 PR 与责任人。

# 常用命令
- 指定端口本地预览：`npm run start -- --port 3003`
- 本地静态预览：`npm run build && npm run serve`
- 链接检查：`npx linkinator build --recurse`

## 22:23 i18n 解释

来源：68ff8052-eb48-8320-9352-5a21f34eaf00
时间：2025-10-27 22:23

### 我
<!-- mid: f22a5141-dcc1-431a-b9d7-67acf5b28701 -->
用 i18n（把中文做默认语言）： 这是什么，

### AI
<!-- mid: d8435c49-3ba0-44de-a818-16beef95a8e7 -->
<details>
<summary>🧠 思考过程 (Thinking Process - 点击展开)</summary>

> **[解释i18n并设置默认中文]**
> i18n是国际化的简称，指的是使软件支持多语言的能力。要在React项目中设置中文为默认语言，可以使用i18next和react-i18next。安装后，配置i18next并添加中文语言包，设置为默认语言即可。
> 
> ```js
> import i18n from 'i18next';
> import { initReactI18next } from 'react-i18next';
> 
> i18n.use(initReactI18next)
>   .init({
>     resources: {
>       en: { translation: { key: "value" } },
>       zh: { translation: { key: "值" } },
>     },
>     lng: "zh", // 设置默认语言为中文
>     interpolation: { escapeValue: false },
>   });
> ```
>
> **[设置i18n并切换语言]**
> 首先，安装i18next和react-i18next等必要的包。初始化时，将默认语言设置为中文并添加资源。以下是配置代码：
> 
> 安装依赖：
> ```bash
> npm install i18next react-i18next
> ```
> 
> i18n配置（`i18n.ts`）：
> ```ts
> import i18n from 'i18next';
> import { initReactI18next } from 'react-i18next';
> 
> i18n.use(initReactI18next).init({
>   resources: {
>     en: { translation: { hello: "Hello world" } },
>     zh: { translation: { hello: "你好，世界" } }
>   },
>   lng: 'zh', // 默认语言
>   interpolation: { escapeValue: false }
> });
> ```
> 
> 语言切换：
> ```ts
> i18n.changeLanguage('en'); // 切换到英文
> i18n.changeLanguage('zh'); // 切换到中文
> ```
> 
> 为保持语言切换状态，可用`localStorage`进行持久化存储。
>
</details>

## 22:50 Card style update

来源：68ff86b2-eb44-8324-898b-a00cbe4d5e7c
时间：2025-10-27 22:50

### 我
<!-- mid: 9b933fb7-db8d-4ee0-a2ea-3eded51193a1 -->
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";

/**
 * LiqPass · 产品页 + 订单管理（React/TSX 单文件）
 *
 * 修复：ReferenceError: isBuyEnabled is not defined
 * 变更：
 * - 定义 `QuoteResp` 接口（被 apiQuote/ProductPage 使用）。
 * - 新增 `isBuyEnabled` 函数，并在自测加入 T7–T9 覆盖购买 gating。
 * - 其余逻辑不变，保持与产品页联动与订单渲染。
 */

// =============================
// 类型
// =============================

type PolicyStatus =
  | "pending_onchain"
  | "active"
  | "expired"
  | "claimed_pending"
  | "claimed_paid"
  | "claimed_denied";

type ChainName = "Base" | string;

type Currency = "USDC" | string;

interface OrderCardData {
  id: string;
  title?: string; // 如 "24h 爆仓保"
  principal: number;
  leverage: number;
  premiumPaid: number; // USDC
  payoutMax: number; // USDC
  status: PolicyStatus | string;
  coverageStartTs: number | string; // 秒/毫秒/ISO 皆可
  coverageEndTs: number | string;   // 秒/毫秒/ISO 皆可
  createdAt: string;                // ISO
  orderRef: string;
  exchangeAccountId?: string;
  chain: ChainName;
  txHash: string;
  orderDigest: string; // 0x...
  skuId?: string;
}

// 产品页联动 payload（与 DemoPage.handleBuy 输出一致）
interface PurchasePayload {
  product: string; // "24h-LiqPass"
  principal: number;
  leverage: number;
  k: number; // 定价系数
  feeRatio: number;   // 保费比例
  feeUSDC: number;    // 保费额
  payoutRatio: number;// 赔付比例
  payoutUSDC: number; // 赔付额
  windowHours: number; // 覆盖时长（小时），如 24
  ts?: number; // 可选，客户端时间戳
}

// 报价响应（后端/Mock）
interface QuoteResp {
  premium: number;
  currency: Currency;
  payoutMax: number;
  load?: number;
  priceExpiresAt: string; // ISO
}

// =============================
// 工具函数
// =============================

const toMs = (t: number | string): number => {
  if (t == null) return NaN;
  if (typeof t === "number") return t < 1e12 ? t * 1000 : t; // 小于 10^12 视为秒
  const n = Number(t);
  if (!Number.isNaN(n)) return n < 1e12 ? n * 1000 : n;
  const d = new Date(t).getTime();
  return Number.isFinite(d) ? d : NaN;
};

const fmtDate = (ts: number | string) => {
  const ms = toMs(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return String(ts);
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("zh-CN", {
    year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
    hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
    hour12: false,
  }).format(new Date(ms));
};

const two = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
const fmtShort = (ts: number | string) => {
  const ms = toMs(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return String(ts);
  const d = new Date(ms);
  return `${two(d.getMonth() + 1)}/${two(d.getDate())} ${two(d.getHours())}:${two(d.getMinutes())}`;
};
const fmtCoverageRange = (start: number | string, end: number | string) => `${fmtShort(start)} → ${fmtShort(end)}`;

const num = (n: number, p = 2) => {
  if (typeof n !== "number" || Number.isNaN(n)) return "-";
  return n.toLocaleString("en-US", { minimumFractionDigits: p, maximumFractionDigits: p });
};

const openChainTx = (chain: ChainName, tx: string) => {
  let url = "";
  if (/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(tx)) {
    if (chain === "Base") url = `https://basescan.org/tx/${tx}`;
  }
  if (!url) url = `https://basescan.org/tx/${tx}`; // 兜底
  window.open(url, "_blank");
};

// 购买按钮可用性判定（修复缺失）
function isBuyEnabled({ wallet, exchangeAccountId, quote }: { wallet?: string; exchangeAccountId?: string; quote?: QuoteResp | null }) {
  const hasWallet = !!wallet && /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{4,}/.test(wallet);
  const hasEacc = !!exchangeAccountId && exchangeAccountId.trim().length > 0;
  const hasQuote = !!quote && typeof quote.premium === "number" && quote.premium > 0;
  const notExpired = !!quote && Date.now() < toMs(quote.priceExpiresAt);
  return Boolean(hasWallet && hasEacc && hasQuote && notExpired);
}

// =============================
// 联动桥：window.liqpass_purchase + localStorage 队列
// =============================

const PURCHASE_KEY = "lp_purchases";

function readPurchases(): PurchasePayload[] {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PURCHASE_KEY) || "[]"); } catch { return []; }
}
function pushPurchase(p: PurchasePayload) {
  const arr = readPurchases();
  arr.push({ ...p, ts: Date.now() });
  localStorage.setItem(PURCHASE_KEY, JSON.stringify(arr));
}
// 暴露全局方法；产品页下单后调用：window.liqpass_purchase(payload)
(function exposeBridge(){
  try {
    (window as any).liqpass_purchase = (p: PurchasePayload) => {
      pushPurchase(p);
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("liqpass:purchase", { detail: p }));
    };
  } catch {}
})();

function mapPurchaseToOrder(p: PurchasePayload): OrderCardData {
  const now = Date.now();
  const start = now;
  const end = now + (Number(p.windowHours || 24) * 3600 * 1000);
  return {
    id: `local_${p.ts ?? now}`,
    title: p.product || "24h 爆仓保",
    principal: Number(p.principal) || 0,
    leverage: Number(p.leverage) || 0,
    premiumPaid: Number(p.feeUSDC) || 0,
    payoutMax: Number(p.payoutUSDC) || 0,
    status: "pending_onchain",
    coverageStartTs: Math.floor(start / 1000),
    coverageEndTs: Math.floor(end / 1000),
    createdAt: new Date(now).toISOString(),
    orderRef: `P-${p.ts ?? now}`,
    exchangeAccountId: undefined,
    chain: "Base",
    txHash: "",
    orderDigest: "0x",
    skuId: "SKU_24H_FIXED",
  };
}

// =============================
// 自测（最小断言）
// =============================

function assert(cond: unknown, msg: string) { if (!cond) throw new Error(msg); }

function runSelfTests() {
  const logs: string[] = [];
  const now = Date.now();

  // T1: toMs 兼容秒/毫秒/ISO
  assert(Math.abs(toMs(now) - now) < 2, "T1a ms passthrough");
  assert(Math.abs(toMs(Math.floor(now / 1000)) - now) < 1100, "T1b sec→ms");
  assert(Math.abs(toMs(new Date(now).toISOString()) - now) < 1100, "T1c iso→ms");
  logs.push("T1 ok");

  // T2: 倒计时为 0 时判定为过期
  const endSoon = Date.now() + 500; // 0.5s
  const remain = Math.max(0, endSoon - Date.now());
  assert(remain >= 0, "T2 remain non-negative");
  const remainAfter = Math.max(0, endSoon - (Date.now() + 1000));
  assert(remainAfter === 0, "T2 reach zero");
  logs.push("T2 ok");

  // T3: 排序（createdAt desc）
  const items = [
    { createdAt: new Date(now - 1000).toISOString() },
    { createdAt: new Date(now - 3000).toISOString() },
    { createdAt: new Date(now - 2000).toISOString() },
  ];
  const sorted = [...items].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt).getTime());
  assert(sorted[0].createdAt > sorted[1].createdAt, "T3 order 0>1");
  assert(sorted[1].createdAt > sorted[2].createdAt, "T3 order 1>2");
  logs.push("T3 ok");

  // T4: 理赔可用性
  const isClaimEnabled = (status: PolicyStatus, end: number) => status === "active" && Date.now() < end;
  assert(isClaimEnabled("active", Date.now() + 10000) === true, "T4 enable active within");
  assert(isClaimEnabled("active", Date.now() - 10000) === false, "T4 disable active expired");
  assert(isClaimEnabled("pending_onchain", Date.now() + 10000) === false, "T4 disable pending_onchain");
  logs.push("T4 ok");

  // T5: 指纹形态
  assert(/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]+$/.test("0xdeadbeef"), "T5 digest shape");
  logs.push("T5 ok");

  // T6: 覆盖窗口短格式
  const s6 = fmtCoverageRange(now, now + 24*3600*1000);
  assert(s6.includes(' → '), 'T6 arrow');
  const colons = (s6.match(/:/g) || []).length;
  assert(colons === 2, 'T6 no seconds');
  logs.push("T6 ok");

  // T7: 报价过期 gating（前端拦截）
  const expiredQuote: QuoteResp = { premium: 3.2, currency: "USDC", payoutMax: 60, priceExpiresAt: new Date(now - 1000).toISOString() };
  const g7 = isBuyEnabled({ wallet: "0xabc123", exchangeAccountId: "eacc_1", quote: expiredQuote });
  assert(g7 === false, "T7 disabled when quote expired");
  logs.push("T7 ok");

  // T8: 必填校验 gating（钱包 / 交易所账号）
  const futureQuote: QuoteResp = { premium: 3.2, currency: "USDC", payoutMax: 60, priceExpiresAt: new Date(now + 60000).toISOString() };
  const g8a = isBuyEnabled({ wallet: "", exchangeAccountId: "eacc_1", quote: futureQuote });
  const g8b = isBuyEnabled({ wallet: "0xabc123", exchangeAccountId: "", quote: futureQuote });
  assert(g8a === false && g8b === false, "T8 disabled when wallet/eacc missing");
  logs.push("T8 ok");

  // T9: 正常启用 gating
  const g9 = isBuyEnabled({ wallet: "0xabc123", exchangeAccountId: "eacc_1", quote: futureQuote });
  assert(g9 === true, "T9 enabled when all present and not expired");
  logs.push("T9 ok");

  // T10: 购买 payload → 订单卡映射
  const p10: PurchasePayload = { product: "24h-LiqPass", principal: 200, leverage: 20, k: 1, feeRatio: 0.05, feeUSDC: 10, payoutRatio: 0.2, payoutUSDC: 40, windowHours: 24 };
  const o10 = mapPurchaseToOrder(p10);
  assert(o10.status === "pending_onchain", "T10 status pending");
  assert(o10.premiumPaid === 10 && o10.payoutMax === 40, "T10 amounts mapped");
  logs.push("T10 ok");

  return { ok: true, logs } as const;
}

// =============================
// Mock（API & 订单）
// =============================

function makeMockOrders(): OrderCardData[] {
  const baseNow = Date.now();
  const mk = (p: Partial<OrderCardData>): OrderCardData => ({
    id: crypto?.randomUUID?.() ?? Math.random().toString(36).slice(2),
    title: "24h 爆仓保",
    principal: 200,
    leverage: 10,
    premiumPaid: 3.2,
    payoutMax: 60,
    status: "active",
    coverageStartTs: Math.floor((baseNow - 60_000) / 1000),
    coverageEndTs: Math.floor((baseNow + 23 * 3600_000 + 59 * 60_000) / 1000),
    createdAt: new Date(baseNow - 5_000).toISOString(),
    orderRef: String(10_000_000_000 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 9_000_000_000)),
    exchangeAccountId: "eacc_mock",
    chain: "Base",
    txHash: "0x" + "a".repeat(64),
    orderDigest: "0x" + "b".repeat(64),
    skuId: "SKU_24H_FIXED",
    ...p,
  });

  return [
    mk({ status: "active", createdAt: new Date(baseNow - 1_000).toISOString() }),
    mk({ status: "pending_onchain", createdAt: new Date(baseNow - 2_000).toISOString() }),
    mk({ status: "claimed_pending", createdAt: new Date(baseNow - 3_000).toISOString() }),
    mk({ status: "claimed_paid", createdAt: new Date(baseNow - 4_000).toISOString() }),
    mk({ status: "expired", coverageEndTs: Math.floor((baseNow - 10_000) / 1000), createdAt: new Date(baseNow - 5_000).toISOString() }),
  ];
}

async function apiQuote(apiBase: string, skuId: string, principal: number, leverage: number): Promise<QuoteResp> {
  const url = apiBase ? `${apiBase.replace(/\/$/, "")}/orders/quote` : "/orders/quote";
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ skuId, principal, leverage }) });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status}`);
    const j = await res.json();
    return j as QuoteResp;
  } catch {
    // Mock 报价：1 分钟过期
    const now = Date.now();
    return { premium: +(principal * 0.016).toFixed(2), currency: "USDC", payoutMax: +(principal * 0.3).toFixed(2), load: 0.12, priceExpiresAt: new Date(now + 60_000).toISOString() };
  }
}

async function apiConfirmOnchain(apiBase: string, txHash: string, priceExpiresAt?: string): Promise<OrderCardData | null> {
  const url = apiBase ? `${apiBase.replace(/\/$/, "")}/orders/confirm-onchain` : "/orders/confirm-onchain";
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Price-Expires-At": priceExpiresAt || "" }, body: JSON.stringify({ txHash }) });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status}`);
    const j = await res.json();
    return j as OrderCardData;
  } catch {
    // Mock：用 txHash 生成一条 active 订单
    const now = Date.now();
    return {
      id: `ord_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)}`,
      title: "24h 爆仓保",
      principal: 200,
      leverage: 10,
      premiumPaid: 3.2,
      payoutMax: 60,
      status: "active",
      coverageStartTs: Math.floor(now / 1000),
      coverageEndTs: Math.floor((now + 24*3600_000) / 1000),
      createdAt: new Date(now).toISOString(),
      orderRef: String(10_000_000_000 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 9_000_000_000)),
      exchangeAccountId: "eacc_mock",
      chain: "Base",
      txHash,
      orderDigest: "0x" + "c".repeat(64),
      skuId: "SKU_24H_FIXED",
    };
  }
}

// =============================
// 顶层：App，包含“产品页/订单页”切换
// =============================

export default function App({ apiBase = "" }: { apiBase?: string }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"product" | "orders">("product");
  const [tests, setTests] = useState<{ ok: boolean; logs: string[]; err?: string }>({ ok: true, logs: [] });

  useEffect(() => { try { setTests(runSelfTests()); } catch (e: any) { setTests({ ok: false, logs: [], err: String(e?.message || e) }); } }, []);

  // 监听来自外部产品页的购买事件 → 跳转订单页
  useEffect(() => {
    const goOrders = () => setTab("orders");
    window.addEventListener("liqpass:purchase", goOrders);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("liqpass:purchase", goOrders);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: "100vh", background: "#FFF7ED", color: "#3F2E20" }}>
      {/* 顶部导航 */}
      <div style={{ position: "sticky", top: 0, zIndex: 40, background: "#FFF7EDF2", borderBottom: "1px solid #e7e5e4" }}>
        <div style={{ maxWidth: 1120, margin: "0 auto", padding: "0 16px", height: 56, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: 12, background: "#fcd34d", border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" }} />
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>LiqPass</div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginLeft: 8 }}>
              <button onClick={() => setTab("product")} style={tabBtn(tab === "product")}>产品页</button>
              <button onClick={() => setTab("orders")} style={tabBtn(tab === "orders")}>订单</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: tests.ok ? "#164e63" : "#7f1d1d" }}>{tests.ok ? `Self-tests: ${tests.logs.join(", ")}` : `Self-tests failed: ${tests.err}`}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {tab === "product" ? <ProductPage apiBase={apiBase} onPurchased={() => setTab("orders")} /> : <OrdersCardsPage apiBase={apiBase} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function tabBtn(active: boolean): React.CSSProperties { return { padding: "6px 10px", borderRadius: 10, background: active ? "#fde68a" : "#fff", border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", cursor: "pointer" }; }

// =============================
// 产品页：参数化 → 报价 → 购买（上链）→ 确认
// =============================

function ProductPage({ apiBase, onPurchased }: { apiBase: string; onPurchased: () => void }) {
  const [skuId, setSkuId] = useState("SKU_24H_FIXED");
  const [principal, setPrincipal] = useState(200);
  const [leverage, setLeverage] = useState(10);
  const [chain, setChain] = useState<ChainName>("Base"); // 预留多链
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState<Currency>("USDC"); // 预留多币

  const [wallet, setWallet] = useState<string>("");
  const [exchangeAccountId, setExchangeAccountId] = useState<string>(""); // 必填

  const [quote, setQuote] = useState<QuoteResp | null>(null);
  const [quoting, setQuoting] = useState(false);
  const [buying, setBuying] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");

  // 过期剩余
  const quoteRemain = quote ? Math.max(0, toMs(quote.priceExpiresAt) - Date.now()) : 0;

  const doQuote = async () => {
    setQuoting(true); setErr("");
    try { const q = await apiQuote(apiBase, skuId, principal, leverage); setQuote(q); }
    catch (e: any) { setErr(String(e?.message || e)); }
    finally { setQuoting(false); }
  };

  // 模拟连接钱包
  const connectWallet = () => { setWallet("0x" + Math.random().toString(16).slice(2, 10) + "...mock"); };

  const buyEnabled = isBuyEnabled({ wallet, exchangeAccountId, quote });

  const BUY = async () => {
    setBuying(true); setErr("");
    try {
      if (!quote || Date.now() >= toMs(quote.priceExpiresAt)) throw new Error("报价已过期"); // 前端拦截
      // 模拟上链：生成 txHash
      const txHash = "0x" + Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32))).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join("");
      localStorage.setItem("pendingTxHash", txHash);
      const order = await apiConfirmOnchain(apiBase, txHash, quote.priceExpiresAt); // 后端强校验由服务完成
      localStorage.removeItem("pendingTxHash");
      if (order) {
        alert("购买成功，跳转订单页");
        onPurchased();
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setErr(String(e?.message || e));
    } finally { setBuying(false); }
  };

  // 异常恢复：若存在 pendingTxHash 则尝试继续确认
  useEffect(() => {
    const pending = localStorage.getItem("pendingTxHash");
    if (pending) {
      (async () => {
        try { await apiConfirmOnchain(apiBase, pending, quote?.priceExpiresAt); }
        finally { localStorage.removeItem("pendingTxHash"); }
      })();
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 760, margin: "16px auto", padding: "0 16px" }}>
      <div style={{ background: "#fff", border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", borderRadius: 12, padding: 16 }}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 12 }}>产品：24h 爆仓保</div>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 12 }}>
          <Labeled label="SKU"> 
            <select value={skuId} onChange={e=>setSkuId(e.target.value)} style={input()}> 
              <option>SKU_24H_FIXED</option>
            </select>
          </Labeled>
          <Labeled label="链/币">
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
              <select value={chain} onChange={e=>setChain(e.target.value)} style={input()}>
                <option>Base</option>
                <option>OtherChain</option>
              </select>
              <select value={currency} onChange={e=>setCurrency(e.target.value)} style={input()}>
                <option>USDC</option>
                <option>OtherToken</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </Labeled>
          <Labeled label="本金(USDC)"><input type="number" value={principal} onChange={e=>setPrincipal(Number(e.target.value))} style={input()} /></Labeled>
          <Labeled label="杠杆(×)"><input type="number" value={leverage} onChange={e=>setLeverage(Number(e.target.value))} style={input()} /></Labeled>
          <Labeled label="钱包">
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
              <input placeholder="0x..." value={wallet} onChange={e=>setWallet(e.target.value)} style={input()} />
              <button onClick={connectWallet} style={btn()}>连接</button>
            </div>
          </Labeled>
          <Labeled label="交易所账号ID（必填）">
            <input placeholder="eacc_..." value={exchangeAccountId} onChange={e=>setExchangeAccountId(e.target.value)} style={input()} />
          </Labeled>
        </div>

        <div style={{ marginTop: 12, display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
          <button onClick={doQuote} disabled={quoting} style={btn(quoting)}>{quoting? '报价中…' : '获取报价'}</button>
          <button onClick={BUY} disabled={!buyEnabled || buying} style={btn(!buyEnabled || buying)}>{buying? '购买中…' : '购买并上链'}</button>
        </div>

        {/* 报价卡 */}
        {quote && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 12, background: '#fffbeb', border: '1px solid #fde68a', padding: 12, borderRadius: 10 }}>
            <div>Premium: <b>${num(quote.premium,2)}</b> · Payout Max: <b>${num(quote.payoutMax,2)}</b> · 币种: {quote.currency}</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#92400e' }}>价格有效期：{fmtShort(quote.priceExpiresAt)}（剩余 {Math.max(0, Math.floor(quoteRemain/1000))}s）</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {err && <div style={{ marginTop: 12, background: '#fee2e2', color: '#7f1d1d', padding: 8, borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 12 }}>{err}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const Labeled: React.FC<{ label: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ label, children }) => (
  <label style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
    <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#52525b' }}>{label}</span>
    {children}
  </label>
);

// =============================
// 订单页：列表卡片
// =============================

function OrdersCardsPage({ apiBase = "" }: { apiBase?: string }) {
  const ORDERS_URL = apiBase ? `${apiBase.replace(/\/$/, "")}/orders` : "/orders";

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>("");
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<OrderCardData[]>([]);

  const refresh = async () => {
    setLoading(true); setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch(ORDERS_URL, { method: "GET" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${res.status} ${res.statusText}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      const list: any[] = Array.isArray(data?.orders) ? data.orders : Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
      const normalized: OrderCardData[] = list.map((r: any, i: number) => ({
        id: r.id ?? r.orderId ?? `${Date.now()}-${i}`,
        title: r.title ?? "24h 爆仓保",
        principal: Number(r.principal ?? 0),
        leverage: Number(r.leverage ?? 0),
        premiumPaid: Number(r.premiumPaid ?? r.premium ?? 0),
        payoutMax: Number(r.payoutMax ?? 0),
        status: String(r.status ?? "active"),
        coverageStartTs: r.coverageStartTs ?? r.coverage_start_ts ?? r.startTs ?? r.start_at,
        coverageEndTs: r.coverageEndTs ?? r.coverage_end_ts ?? r.endTs ?? r.end_at,
        createdAt: r.createdAt ?? r.created_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
        orderRef: r.orderRef ?? r.order_ref ?? "",
        exchangeAccountId: r.exchangeAccountId ?? r.exchange_account_id,
        chain: r.chain ?? "Base",
        txHash: r.txHash ?? r.tx_hash ?? "",
        orderDigest: r.orderDigest ?? r.order_digest ?? "",
        skuId: r.skuId ?? r.sku_id ?? "SKU_24H_FIXED",
      }));

      // 合并本地购买（pending_onchain）在前
      const locals = readPurchases().map(mapPurchaseToOrder);
      const merged = [...locals, ...normalized];

      // 倒序
      merged.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt).getTime());
      setRows(merged);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.warn("/orders failed, fallback to mock:", e?.message || e);
      setError("订单服务不可用，展示演示数据");
      const mock = makeMockOrders();
      const locals = readPurchases().map(mapPurchaseToOrder);
      const merged = [...locals, ...mock].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt).getTime());
      setRows(merged);
    } finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  useEffect(() => { refresh(); /* eslint-disable-next-line */ }, [apiBase]);

  // 监听 localStorage 与自定义事件，动态刷新
  useEffect(() => {
    const onPurchase = () => refresh();
    const onStorage = (e: StorageEvent) => { if (e.key === PURCHASE_KEY) refresh(); };
    window.addEventListener("liqpass:purchase", onPurchase);
    window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
    return () => { window.removeEventListener("liqpass:purchase", onPurchase); window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage); };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 1120, margin: "8px auto 24px", padding: "0 16px", display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
      {loading && <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#92400e" }}>加载中</div>}
      {error && <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#7f1d1d" }}>{error}</div>}
      {rows.map((o) => (<OrderCard key={o.id} data={o} />))}
      {rows.length === 0 && (<div style={{ padding: 24, background: "#fff", border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", borderRadius: 12, color: "#71717a" }}>暂无订单</div>)}
    </div>
  );
}

// =============================
// 订单卡组件
// =============================

const OrderCard: React.FC<{ data: OrderCardData; variant?: CardVariant }> = ({ data, variant = "receipt" }) => {
  const { principal, leverage, premiumPaid, payoutMax, status, coverageStartTs, coverageEndTs, createdAt, orderRef, exchangeAccountId, chain, txHash, title } = data;

  const startMs = toMs(coverageStartTs);
  const endMs = toMs(coverageEndTs);
  const now = Date.now();
  const remainMs = Math.max(0, endMs - now);
  const isExpiredUi = remainMs <= 0 || status === "expired";
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (now - startMs) / Math.max(1, endMs - startMs)));

  const badge = (s: string) => {
    const map: Record<string, { t: string; bg: string; fg: string }> = {
      pending_onchain: { t: "上链确认中", bg: "#fef9c3", fg: "#854d0e" },
      active: { t: "生效中", bg: "#dcfce7", fg: "#065f46" },
      expired: { t: "已过期", bg: "#e5e7eb", fg: "#374151" },
      claimed_pending: { t: "理赔审核", bg: "#dbeafe", fg: "#1e3a8a" },
      claimed_paid: { t: "已赔付", bg: "#dcfce7", fg: "#065f46" },
      claimed_denied: { t: "理赔拒绝", bg: "#fee2e2", fg: "#7f1d1d" },
    };
    const v = map[s] || { t: s, bg: "#e5e7eb", fg: "#374151" };
    return <span style={{ background: v.bg, color: v.fg, padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 12 }}>{v.t}</span>;
  };

  const fmtRemain = (ms: number) => {
    const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
    const hh = String(Math.floor(s / 3600)).padStart(2, "0");
    const mm = String(Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
    const ss = String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
    return `T-${hh}:${mm}:${ss}`;
  };

  const claimEnabled = status === "active" && !isExpiredUi;

  const Actions = (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
      <button style={btn()} onClick={() => openChainTx(chain, txHash)}>查看链上</button>
      <button style={btn(!claimEnabled)} disabled={!claimEnabled} onClick={() => onClaimClick(data)}>发起理赔</button>
      <button style={btn()} onClick={() => onDetailClick(data)}>详情</button>
    </div>
  );

  const Chips = (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
      <SmallKV k="Principal" v={`$${num(principal, 2)}`} />
      <SmallKV k="Leverage" v={`${leverage}×`} />
      <SmallKV k="Premium" v={`$${num(premiumPaid, 2)}`} />
      <SmallKV k="Payout Max" v={`$${num(payoutMax, 2)}`} />
    </div>
  );

  const InfoLine = (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr))", gap: 8, fontSize: 13, color: "#52525b" }}>
      <Field label="购买时间" value={fmtDate(createdAt)} />
      <Field label="覆盖窗口" value={<span style={{whiteSpace:'nowrap',fontFamily:'ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace',background:'#f8fafc',border:'1px solid #e5e7eb',borderRadius:8,padding:'2px 8px'}}>{fmtCoverageRange(coverageStartTs, coverageEndTs)}</span>} />
      <Field label="订单号" value={orderRef?.slice(-8)} mono />
      <Field label="最近校验账号" value={exchangeAccountId || "-"} mono />
    </div>
  );

  const RightStatus = (
    <div style={{ display:'grid', justifyItems:'end', gap:8, minWidth:160 }}>
      <div>{badge(isExpiredUi ? "expired" : String(status))}</div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily:'ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace', fontSize:18, color:'#0f172a' }}>{isExpiredUi ? "T-00:00:00" : fmtRemain(remainMs)}</div>
    </div>
  );

  if (variant === "receipt") {
    return (
      <div style={{ background: "#fff", border: "1px solid #e
    const v = map[s] || { t: s, bg: "#e5e7eb", fg: "#374151" };
    return <span style={{ background: v.bg, color: v.fg, padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 12 }}>{v.t}</span>;
  };

  const fmtRemain = (ms: number) => {
    const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
    const hh = String(Math.floor(s / 3600)).padStart(2, "0");
    const mm = String(Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
    const ss = String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
    return `T-${hh}:${mm}:${ss}`;
  };

  const claimEnabled = status === "active" && !isExpiredUi;

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#fff", border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", borderRadius: 12, padding: 16 }}>
      {/* 顶部 */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{title || "24h 爆仓保"}</div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <SmallKV k="Principal" v={`$${num(principal, 2)}`} />
            <SmallKV k="Leverage" v={`${leverage}×`} />
            <SmallKV k="Premium" v={`$${num(premiumPaid, 2)}`} />
            <SmallKV k="Payout Max" v={`$${num(payoutMax, 2)}`} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
          {badge(isExpiredUi ? "expired" : String(status))}
          <span style={{ fontFamily: "ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace", fontSize: 13, color: "#0f172a" }}>{isExpiredUi ? "T-00:00:00" : fmtRemain(remainMs)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 次要信息 */}
      <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr))", gap: 8, fontSize: 13, color: "#52525b" }}>
        <Field label="购买时间" value={fmtDate(createdAt)} />
        <Field label="覆盖窗口" value={<span style={{whiteSpace:'nowrap',fontFamily:'ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace',background:'#f8fafc',border:'1px solid #e5e7eb',borderRadius:8,padding:'2px 8px'}}>{fmtCoverageRange(coverageStartTs, coverageEndTs)}</span>} />
        <Field label="订单号" value={orderRef?.slice(-8)} mono />
        <Field label="最近校验账号" value={exchangeAccountId || "-"} mono />
      </div>

      {/* 动作 */}
      <div style={{ marginTop: 12, display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <button style={btn()} onClick={() => openChainTx(chain, txHash)}>查看链上</button>
        <button style={btn(!claimEnabled)} disabled={!claimEnabled} onClick={() => onClaimClick(data)}>发起理赔</button>
        <button style={btn()} onClick={() => onDetailClick(data)}>详情</button>
      </div>

      {/* 原始 JSON（调试用，生产可隐藏） */}
      <details style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
        <summary style={{ cursor: "pointer", color: "#6b7280" }}>原始 JSON</summary>
        <pre style={{ marginTop: 8, background: "#0a0a0a", color: "#e5e5e5", padding: 12, borderRadius: 10, overflow: "auto", fontSize: 12 }}>{JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}</pre>
      </details>
    </div>
  );
};

function onClaimClick(o: OrderCardData) {
  alert(`发起理赔（占位）：orderId=${o.id}, orderRef=${o.orderRef}`);
}

function onDetailClick(o: OrderCardData) {
  alert(`详情（占位）: ${o.id}`);
}

// =============================
// UI 小部件与样式
// =============================

const SmallKV: React.FC<{ k: string; v: string }> = ({ k, v }) => (
  <span style={{ background: "#f8fafc", border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", borderRadius: 8, padding: "2px 8px", fontSize: 12 }}>
    <b style={{ color: "#334155" }}>{k}</b> <span style={{ color: "#0f172a" }}>{v}</span>
  </span>
);

const Field: React.FC<{ label: string; value: React.ReactNode; mono?: boolean }> = ({ label, value, mono }) => (
  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
    <span style={{ color: "#737373", whiteSpace: "nowrap", wordBreak: "keep-all" }}>{label}</span>
    <span style={{ fontFamily: mono ? "ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace" : undefined }}>{value}</span>
  </div>
);

function btn(disabled = false): React.CSSProperties { return { padding: "8px 12px", borderRadius: 10, background: disabled ? "#f5f5f5" : "#ffffff", border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", cursor: disabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer" }; }
function input(): React.CSSProperties { return { padding: "8px 12px", borderRadius: 10, border: "1px solid #e5e7eb", background: "#fff", width: "100%" }; } 修改卡片的样式

<!-- mid: 3c067a3d-aa1f-44d7-a689-c2f464e15e8a -->
<details>
<summary>🧠 思考过程 (Thinking Process - 点击展开)</summary>

> **[Preparing production-ready design]**
> For production, using CSS classes with Tailwind might be better, but since it's just a single TSX file with inline styles and no CSS framework, we'll stick to inline for now.
> 
> My plan:
> - Introduce color tokens.
> - Redesign the Card component with subtle shadow, hover transform, and progress bar.
> - Replace small chips with a neat grid for metrics.
> - Group action buttons on the right, with claim if enabled.
> - Use monospace for timers.
>
> **[Refining the OrderCard component]**
> It looks like there's duplication in the OrderCard component, especially with the "receipt" variant logic being repeated. To maintain consistency, we should clean up the code.
> 
> I’m considering whether to implement both "receipt" and "list" variants or unify them into one improved design. We also need to fix the "isBuyEnabled" issue. But since the focus is on modifying the card style, I’ll avoid overcomplicating it.
>
> **[Creating full React component for preview]**
